Each\n// MPI rank owns a contiguous band of tile rows and keeps it resident on its GPU.\n// For every pivot block kb the owning rank computes the pivot tile (phase 1) and\n// the pivot tile row (phase 2, row part) and broadcasts that 64 x N strip to all\n// ranks. Every rank then updates the pivot tile column of its band (phase 2,\n// column part) and all remaining tiles of its band (phase 3). The owner of the\n// next pivot strip computes it first (look-ahead), so the broadcast of strip\n// kb+1 overlaps with the bulk phase-3 work of iteration kb on all GPUs.\n// OpenMP is used for host-side initialization (with an exact jump-ahead of the\n// rand_r sequence) and validation.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Tile size and thread-block geometry (each thread handles 4x4 elements)\nconstexpr int TILE = 64;\nconstexpr int TDIM = 16;\nconstexpr int PER = TILE / TDIM;\nconstexpr unsigned int NO_UPDATE = 0xFFFFFFFFu;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                          \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Initialization\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Seed of rand_r after `calls` invocations, assuming the glibc LCG\n// (3 steps of next = next * 1103515245 + 12345 per call).\nstatic unsigned int randrJump(unsigned int seed, uint64_t calls) {\n    uint64_t steps = calls * 3;\n    unsigned int accMul = 1, accAdd = 0;\n    unsigned int mul = 1103515245u, add = 12345u;\n    while (steps) {\n        if (steps & 1) {\n            accMul *= mul;\n            accAdd = accAdd * mul + add;\n        }\n        add = add * mul + add;\n        mul *= mul;\n        steps >>= 1;\n    }\n    return seed * accMul + accAdd;\n}\n\nstatic bool randrJumpSupported() {\n    unsigned int s = 42;\n    for (int c = 1; c <= 64; ++c) {\n        rand_r(&s);\n        if (s != randrJump(42, c)) return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Fill rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of the distance matrix (row-major, leading\n// dimension ld, padding = INF). Produces exactly the same values as the\n// sequential rand_r-based initialization over the whole numNodes^2 matrix.\nvoid initializeDistanceRows(unsigned int* local, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld,\n                            const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                            const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t realEnd = std::min(rowEnd, numNodes);\n    const size_t nLocalRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < nLocalRows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = local + r * ld;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ld; ++c) row[c] = INF;\n    }\n    if (realEnd <= rowBegin) return;\n\n    const size_t first = rowBegin * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = realEnd * numNodes;\n\n    if (randrJumpSupported()) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            const size_t nt = omp_get_num_threads(), t = omp_get_thread_num();\n            const size_t total = last - first;\n            const size_t b = first + total * t / nt, e = first + total * (t + 1) / nt;\n            unsigned int seed = randrJump(42, b);\n            for (size_t i = b; i < e; ++i) {\n                const unsigned int v =\n                    rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n                local[(i / numNodes - rowBegin) * ld + i % numNodes] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        unsigned int seed = 42;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) rand_r(&seed);\n        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n            const unsigned int v =\n                rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            local[(i / numNodes - rowBegin) * ld + i % numNodes] = v;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < realEnd; ++i) local[(i - rowBegin) * ld + i] = 0;\n}\n\n// Path matrix initial state: path[i][j] = i (row index).\n__global__ void initPathKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ path, size_t ld, size_t rows,\n                               unsigned int rowBegin) {\n    const size_t total = rows * ld;\n    for (size_t e = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; e < total;\n         e += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x)\n        path[e] = rowBegin + (unsigned int)(e / ld);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Blocked Floyd-Warshall kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Phases 1 and 2 (tiles depending on themselves).\n//   MODE 0: pivot tile          C = C (x) C\n//   MODE 1: pivot-row tile      C = D (x) C   (D = pivot tile)\n//   MODE 2: pivot-column tile   C = C (x) D\n// For MODE 1 tiles are indexed by blockIdx.x as column tiles of the pivot strip;\n// for MODE 2 by blockIdx.x as local row tiles. Tile `skip` is not processed.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TDIM* TDIM)\n    fwDependentKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ diag, size_t ld, int kb, int skip,\n                      int tileOffset) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int sS[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ unsigned int sD[MODE == 0 ? 1 : TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TDIM + tx;\n    const int t = blockIdx.x;\n    if (MODE != 0 && t + tileOffset == skip) return;\n\n    size_t rowOff, colOff;\n    if (MODE == 0) {\n        rowOff = 0;\n        colOff = (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    } else if (MODE == 1) {\n        rowOff = 0;\n        colOff = (size_t)t * TILE;\n    } else {\n        rowOff = (size_t)t * TILE;\n        colOff = (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    }\n    unsigned int* C = dist + rowOff * ld + colOff;\n    unsigned int* P = path + rowOff * ld + colOff;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int q = 0; q < TILE * TILE / (TDIM * TDIM); ++q) {\n        const int e = tid + q * TDIM * TDIM;\n        const int r = e / TILE, c = e % TILE;\n        sS[r][c] = C[r * ld + c];\n        if (MODE != 0) sD[r][c] = diag[r * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE + c];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int cur[PER][PER], pth[PER][PER];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            cur[i][j] = sS[ty + TDIM * i][tx + TDIM * j];\n            pth[i][j] = NO_UPDATE;\n        }\n\n    const unsigned int kBase = (unsigned int)kb * TILE;\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        unsigned int a[PER], b[PER];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i) a[i] = (MODE == 1) ? sD[ty + TDIM * i][k] : sS[ty + TDIM * i][k];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) b[j] = (MODE == 2) ? sD[k][tx + TDIM * j] : sS[k][tx + TDIM * j];\n        // Row k / column k of the self tile never change during step k, so\n        // in-place updates here do not race with the reads above.\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int nd = a[i] + b[j];\n                if (nd < cur[i][j]) {\n                    cur[i][j] = nd;\n                    pth[i][j] = kBase + k;\n                    sS[ty + TDIM * i][tx + TDIM * j] = nd;\n                }\n            }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            const size_t o = (size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j;\n            C[o] = cur[i][j];\n            if (pth[i][j] != NO_UPDATE) P[o] = pth[i][j];\n        }\n}\n\n// Phase 3: all tiles not in the pivot row/column of the local band.\n// blockIdx.x = column tile, blockIdx.y = local row tile.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TDIM* TDIM)\n    fwPhase3Kernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                   const unsigned int* __restrict__ strip, size_t ld, int kb,\n                   int tileOffset, int skipRow) {\n    const int bx = blockIdx.x, by = blockIdx.y;\n    const int gRow = by + tileOffset;\n    if (bx == kb || gRow == kb || gRow == skipRow) return;\n\n    __shared__ unsigned int sA[TILE][TILE + 1];  // dist[i][k]\n    __shared__ unsigned int sB[TILE][TILE];      // dist[k][j]\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TDIM + tx;\n\n    const unsigned int* A = dist + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    const unsigned int* Bm = strip + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int q = 0; q < TILE * TILE / (TDIM * TDIM); ++q) {\n        const int e = tid + q * TDIM * TDIM;\n        const int r = e / TILE, c = e % TILE;\n        sA[r][c] = A[r * ld + c];\n        sB[r][c] = Bm[r * ld + c];\n    }\n\n    unsigned int* C = dist + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n    unsigned int* P = path + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n    unsigned int cur[PER][PER], pth[PER][PER];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            cur[i][j] = C[(size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j];\n            pth[i][j] = NO_UPDATE;\n        }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const unsigned int kBase = (unsigned int)kb * TILE;\n#pragma unroll 8\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        unsigned int a[PER], b[PER];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i) a[i] = sA[ty + TDIM * i][k];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) b[j] = sB[k][tx + TDIM * j];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int nd = a[i] + b[j];\n                if (nd < cur[i][j]) {\n                    cur[i][j] = nd;\n                    pth[i][j] = kBase + k;\n                }\n            }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            if (pth[i][j] != NO_UPDATE) {\n                const size_t o = (size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j;\n                C[o] = cur[i][j];\n                P[o] = pth[i][j];\n            }\n        }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Distributed driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct Band {\n    int rank, size;\n    int numTiles;         // tiles per dimension (padded)\n    size_t ld;            // padded dimension\n    int tileBegin;        // first global tile row owned\n    int tileCount;        // number of tile rows owned\n    std::vector<int> tileStarts;  // per rank\n};\n\nstatic int ownerOf(const Band& b, int tile) {\n    int r = (int)(std::upper_bound(b.tileStarts.begin(), b.tileStarts.end(), tile) -\n                  b.tileStarts.begin()) - 1;\n    while (r + 1 < b.size && b.tileStarts[r + 1] == tile) ++r;  // skip empty ranks\n    return r;\n}\n\n// Runs the blocked algorithm on device-resident band (dDist/dPath).\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dDist, unsigned int* dPath, const Band& b,\n                   cudaStream_t stream) {\n    const int nt = b.numTiles;\n    const size_t ld = b.ld;\n    const size_t stripElems = (size_t)TILE * ld;\n    const dim3 block(TDIM, TDIM);\n    const bool multi = b.size > 1;\n\n    unsigned int* dStrip[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    unsigned int* hStrip[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaEvent_t h2dDone[2], d2hDone;\n    if (multi) {\n        for (int i = 0; Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t m = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    // (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF && distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                    firstBad = std::min(firstBad, (i * m + j) * numNodes + k);\n                    break;\n                }\n Each\n// MPI rank owns a contiguous band of tile rows and keeps it resident on its GPU.\n// For every pivot block kb the owning rank computes the pivot tile (phase 1) and\n// the pivot tile row (phase 2, row part) and broadcasts that 64 x N strip to all\n// ranks. Every rank then updates the pivot tile column of its band (phase 2,\n// column part) and all remaining tiles of its band (phase 3). The owner of the\n// next pivot strip computes it first (look-ahead), so the broadcast of strip\n// kb+1 overlaps with the bulk phase-3 work of iteration kb on all GPUs.\n// OpenMP is used for host-side initialization (with an exact jump-ahead of the\n// rand_r sequence) and validation.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Tile size and thread-block geometry (each thread handles 4x4 elements)\nconstexpr int TILE = 64;\nconstexpr int TDIM = 16;\nconstexpr int PER = TILE / TDIM;\nconstexpr unsigned int NO_UPDATE = 0xFFFFFFFFu;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                          \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Initialization\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Seed of rand_r after `calls` invocations, assuming the glibc LCG\n// (3 steps of next = next * 1103515245 + 12345 per call).\nstatic unsigned int randrJump(unsigned int seed, uint64_t calls) {\n    uint64_t steps = calls * 3;\n    unsigned int accMul = 1, accAdd = 0;\n    unsigned int mul = 1103515245u, add = 12345u;\n    while (steps) {\n        if (steps & 1) {\n            accMul *= mul;\n            accAdd = accAdd * mul + add;\n        }\n        add = add * mul + add;\n        mul *= mul;\n        steps >>= 1;\n    }\n    return seed * accMul + accAdd;\n}\n\nstatic bool randrJumpSupported() {\n    unsigned int s = 42;\n    for (int c = 1; c <= 64; ++c) {\n        rand_r(&s);\n        if (s != randrJump(42, c)) return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Fill rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of the distance matrix (row-major, leading\n// dimension ld, padding = INF). Produces exactly the same values as the\n// sequential rand_r-based initialization over the whole numNodes^2 matrix.\nvoid initializeDistanceRows(unsigned int* local, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld,\n                            const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                            const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t realEnd = std::min(rowEnd, numNodes);\n    const size_t nLocalRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < nLocalRows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = local + r * ld;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ld; ++c) row[c] = INF;\n    }\n    if (realEnd <= rowBegin) return;\n\n    const size_t first = rowBegin * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = realEnd * numNodes;\n\n    if (randrJumpSupported()) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            const size_t nt = omp_get_num_threads(), t = omp_get_thread_num();\n            const size_t total = last - first;\n            const size_t b = first + total * t / nt, e = first + total * (t + 1) / nt;\n            unsigned int seed = randrJump(42, b);\n            for (size_t i = b; i < e; ++i) {\n                const unsigned int v =\n                    rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n                local[(i / numNodes - rowBegin) * ld + i % numNodes] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        unsigned int seed = 42;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) rand_r(&seed);\n        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n            const unsigned int v =\n                rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            local[(i / numNodes - rowBegin) * ld + i % numNodes] = v;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < realEnd; ++i) local[(i - rowBegin) * ld + i] = 0;\n}\n\n// Path matrix initial state: path[i][j] = i (row index).\n__global__ void initPathKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ path, size_t ld, size_t rows,\n                               unsigned int rowBegin) {\n    const size_t total = rows * ld;\n    for (size_t e = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; e < total;\n         e += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x)\n        path[e] = rowBegin + (unsigned int)(e / ld);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Blocked Floyd-Warshall kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Phases 1 and 2 (tiles depending on themselves).\n//   MODE 0: pivot tile          C = C (x) C\n//   MODE 1: pivot-row tile      C = D (x) C   (D = pivot tile)\n//   MODE 2: pivot-column tile   C = C (x) D\n// For MODE 1 tiles are indexed by blockIdx.x as column tiles of the pivot strip;\n// for MODE 2 by blockIdx.x as local row tiles. Tile `skip` is not processed.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TDIM* TDIM)\n    fwDependentKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ diag, size_t ld, int kb, int skip,\n                      int tileOffset) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int sS[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ unsigned int sD[MODE == 0 ? 1 : TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TDIM + tx;\n    const int t = blockIdx.x;\n    if (MODE != 0 && t + tileOffset == skip) return;\n\n    size_t rowOff, colOff;\n    if (MODE == 0) {\n        rowOff = 0;\n        colOff = (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    } else if (MODE == 1) {\n        rowOff = 0;\n        colOff = (size_t)t * TILE;\n    } else {\n        rowOff = (size_t)t * TILE;\n        colOff = (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    }\n    unsigned int* C = dist + rowOff * ld + colOff;\n    unsigned int* P = path + rowOff * ld + colOff;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int q = 0; q < TILE * TILE / (TDIM * TDIM); ++q) {\n        const int e = tid + q * TDIM * TDIM;\n        const int r = e / TILE, c = e % TILE;\n        sS[r][c] = C[r * ld + c];\n        if (MODE != 0) sD[r][c] = diag[r * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE + c];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int cur[PER][PER], pth[PER][PER];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            const size_t o = (size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j;\n            C[o] = cur[i][j];\n            if (pth[i][j] != NO_UPDATE) P[o] = pth[i][j];\n        }\n}\n\n// Phase 3: all tiles not in the pivot row/column of the local band.\n// blockIdx.x = column tile, blockIdx.y = local row tile.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TDIM* TDIM)\n    fwPhase3Kernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                   const unsigned int* __restrict__ strip, size_t ld, int kb,\n                   int tileOffset, int skipRow) {\n    const int bx = blockIdx.x, by = blockIdx.y;\n    const int gRow = by + tileOffset;\n    if (bx == kb || gRow == kb || gRow == skipRow) return;\n\n    __shared__ unsigned int sA[TILE][TILE + 1];  // dist[i][k]\n    __shared__ unsigned int sB[TILE][TILE];      // dist[k][j]\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TDIM + tx;\n\n    const unsigned int* A = dist + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    const unsigned int* Bm = strip + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int q = 0; q < TILE * TILE / (TDIM * TDIM); ++q) {\n        const int e = tid + q * TDIM * TDIM;\n        const int r = e / TILE, c = e % TILE;\n        sA[r][c] = A[r * ld + c];\n        sB[r][c] = Bm[r * ld + c];\n    }\n\n    unsigned int* C = dist + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n    unsigned int* P = path + (size_t)by * TILE * ld + (size_t)bx * TILE;\n    unsigned int cur[PER][PER], pth[PER][PER];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            cur[i][j] = C[(size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j];\n            pth[i][j] = NO_UPDATE;\n        }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const unsigned int kBase = (unsigned int)kb * TILE;\n#pragma unroll 8\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        unsigned int a[PER], b[PER];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i) a[i] = sA[ty + TDIM * i][k];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) b[j] = sB[k][tx + TDIM * j];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int nd = a[i] + b[j];\n                if (nd < cur[i][j]) {\n                    cur[i][j] = nd;\n                    pth[i][j] = kBase + k;\n                }\n            }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < PER; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < PER; ++j) {\n            if (pth[i][j] != NO_UPDATE) {\n                const size_t o = (size_t)(ty + TDIM * i) * ld + tx + TDIM * j;\n                C[o] = cur[i][j];\n                P[o] = pth[i][j];\n            }\n        }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Distributed driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct Band {\n    int rank, size;\n    int numTiles;         // tiles per dimension (padded)\n    size_t ld;            // padded dimension\n    int tileBegin;        // first global tile row owned\n    int tileCount;        // number of tile rows owned\n    std::vector<int> tileStarts;  // per rank\n};\n\nstatic int ownerOf(const Band& b, int tile) {\n    int r = (int)(std::upper_bound(b.tileStarts.begin(), b.tileStarts.end(), tile) -\n                  b.tileStarts.begin()) - 1;\n    while (r + 1 < b.size && b.tileStarts[r + 1] == tile) ++r; i < 2; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dStrip[i], stripElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hStrip[i], stripElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&h2dDone[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&d2hDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    }\n\n    auto ownsTile = [&](int t) { return t >= b.tileBegin && t < b.tileBegin + b.tileCount; };\n    auto localRow = [&](int t) { return dDist + (size_t)(t - b.tileBegin) * stripElems; };\n    auto localPath = [&](int t) { return dPath + (size_t)(t - b.tileBegin) * stripElems; };\n\n    // Phase 1 + phase 2 (row part) for pivot block kb on its owner; then\n    // stage it for broadcast.\n    auto computePivotStrip = [&](int kb) {\n        unsigned int* d = localRow(kb);\n        unsigned int* p = localPath(kb);\n        fwDependentKernel<0><<<1, block, 0, stream>>>(d, p, d, ld, kb, -1, 0);\n        fwDependentKernel<1><<<nt, block, 0, stream>>>(d, p, d, ld, kb, kb, 0);\n        if (multi) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hStrip[kb & 1], d, stripElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(d2hDone, stream));\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Broadcast strip kb; returns the device pointer holding it.\n    auto shareStrip = [&](int kb) -> const unsigned int* {\n        if (!multi) return localRow(kb);\n        const int root = ownerOf(b, kb);\n        const int buf = kb & 1;\n        if (b.rank == root) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(d2hDone));\n            MPI_Bcast(hStrip[buf], (int)stripElems, MPI_UNSIGNED, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            return localRow(kb);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(h2dDone[buf]));\n        MPI_Bcast(hStrip[buf], (int)stripElems, MPI_UNSIGNED, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dStrip[buf], hStrip[buf], stripElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(h2dDone[buf], stream));\n        return dStrip[buf];\n    };\n\n    if (ownsTile(0)) computePivotStrip(0);\n    const unsigned int* strip = shareStrip(0);\n\n    for (int kb = 0; kb < nt; ++kb) {\n        if (b.tileCount > 0) {\n            // Phase 2, column part, on the local band\n            fwDependentKernel<2><<<b.tileCount, block, 0, stream>>>(\n                dDist, dPath, strip, ld, kb, kb, b.tileBegin);\n\n            const int next = kb + 1;\n            int skipRow = -1;\n            if (next < nt && ownsTile(next)) {\n                // Look-ahead: finish the next pivot strip first\n                fwPhase3Kernel<<<dim3(nt, 1), block, 0, stream>>>(\n                    localRow(next), localPath(next), strip, ld, kb, next, -1);\n                computePivotStrip(next);\n                skipRow = next;\n            }\n            fwPhase3Kernel<<<dim3(nt, b.tileCount), block, 0, stream>>>(\n                dDist, dPath, strip, ld, kb, b.tileBegin, skipRow);\n        }\n        if (kb + 1 < nt) strip = shareStrip(kb + 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n    if (multi) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dStrip[i]));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hStrip[i]));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(h2dDone[i]));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(d2hDone));\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t m = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    // Find the first violation (in sequential order) in parallel\n    size_t firstBad = SIZE_MAX;\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(min : firstBad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < m; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF && distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                    firstBad = std::min(firstBad, (i * m + j) * numNodes + k);\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    if (firstBad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        const size_t k = firstBad % numNodes, ij = firstBad / numNodes;\n        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n               ij / m, ij % m, k);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfkQ6dsQ2nd9qVKWbgAoV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The Floyd-Warshall benchmark now runs as hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA, and its output matches the original's exactly. The distance matrix checksum and hash were identical for n = 1, 63, 100, 512, 1000, 2048 on 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 and 8 ranks, and for n = 3000 on 4 ranks. Validation passes in every case.\n\nThe changes are in `floydwarshall.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` only; the executable is still `floydwarshall`.\n\n**How the work is split**\n- **CUDA:** the matrix is cut into 64×64 tiles and processed in the standard blocked three-phase order, with each thread computing 4×4 entries from shared memory. The path matrix is updated on the GPU only where a distance improves.\n- **MPI:** each rank owns a band of rows on its own GPU. At each step, the rank owning the next pivot rows computes them first and sends them out while all GPUs carry on with the bulk of the current step.\n- **Communication:** ranks on the same node copy pivot rows GPU-to-GPU via CUDA IPC, which is part of the CUDA runtime, so no new dependency. This works without direct GPU-to-GPU support, which these RTX 3090s lack. Between nodes, the rows go through host memory with `MPI_Bcast`. If nodes have different rank counts, or IPC can't be set up, it falls back to plain MPI.\n- **Result collection:** rank 0 pulls results from GPUs on its own node over IPC; other nodes send theirs with `MPI_Gatherv`.\n- **OpenMP:** the random initialization runs in parallel and reproduces the original `rand_r` sequence exactly, falling back to the serial loop if the C library's `rand_r` behaves differently. Validation also runs in parallel.\n- **CMake:** the `.cpp` is compiled as CUDA, and the build links MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA runtime.\n\n**Performance** (n=16384; the original serial code takes hours at this size)\n\n| GPUs | Time | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 2048 ms | 1.0× |\n| 2 | 1133 ms | 1.8× |\n| 4 | 780 ms | 2.6× |\n\nThe 4-GPU result is limited by this machine's MPI. It isn't CUDA-aware and moves data between processes at only about 1–2 GB/s, which is why I added the IPC paths. A plain MPI broadcast version took 1338 ms on 4 GPUs. At n=8192 on 4 GPUs it reaches about 4 TOPS.\n\n**Things that behave differently**\n- **Path matrix:** distances are identical, but the path matrix can differ from the original when two routes are equally short, because the blocked algorithm processes updates in a different order. The program never prints or validates it.\n- **Timed region:** the reported time now also covers copying the input to the GPUs and collecting both matrices on rank 0. Setup (allocation, IPC handle exchange, pinning host memory) happens before the timer starts.\n\n**Not tested on real hardware:** I've only run this on one machine with 4 GPUs. I checked the multi-node and fallback code by temporarily forcing them (a simulated 2-node layout and an all-MPI layout), and both passed the full test sweep. They haven't run on an actual multi-node cluster."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":888,"cache_read_input_tokens":114725,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":888},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd9e31f1-957f-47ba-b661-a6597497918f","uuid":"38b3a895-c6c2-437b-b9eb-f497005eaec5","timestamp":"2026-10-06T05:04:03.867Z","request_id":"req_011CfkQ6d5WW6vs9Z42uhki9"}
